Mathlib.AlgebraicGeometry.AffineScheme

AlgebraicGeometry.AffineSchemedefMathlib.AlgebraicGeometry.AffineScheme, type
Wording
affine scheme (from the name)
English
An affine scheme is an essential image subcategory of the spectrum.
Formula
$\operatorname{AffineScheme} := \operatorname{EssImageSubcategory} (\operatorname{Spec})$
AlgebraicGeometry.AffineScheme.SpecdefMathlib.AlgebraicGeometry.AffineScheme, function or constant
Wording
the spectrum (from the name)
English
The spectrum is a functor of the set of opposites of the set of categories of commutative rings and the set of affine schemes defined as the essential image of the spectrum.
Formula
$\operatorname{Spec} := \operatorname{toEssImage} (\operatorname{Spec})\in \operatorname{Functor} (\mathrm{Opposite} (\mathrm{CommRingCat}), \operatorname{AffineScheme})$
AlgebraicGeometry.AffineScheme.equivCommRingCatdefMathlib.AlgebraicGeometry.AffineScheme, function or constant
Wording
the equivalence commutative ring category (from the name)
English
The equivalence commutative ring category is an equivalence of the set of affine schemes and the set of opposites of the set of categories of commutative rings defined as the inverse of the equivalence essential image.
Formula
$\operatorname{equivCommRingCat} := \operatorname{symm} (\operatorname{equivEssImageOfReflective})\in \operatorname{Equivalence} (\operatorname{AffineScheme} , \mathrm{Opposite} (\mathrm{CommRingCat}))$
AlgebraicGeometry.AffineScheme.forgetToSchemedefMathlib.AlgebraicGeometry.AffineScheme, function or constant
Wording
the forgetful functor (from the docstring)
English
The forgetful functor is a functor of the set of affine schemes and the set of schemes defined as the inclusion of the essential image of the spectrum.
Formula
$\operatorname{forgetToScheme} := \mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_ObjectProperty\_\_\_u3b9\_} (\operatorname{essImage} (\operatorname{Spec}))\in \operatorname{Functor} (\operatorname{AffineScheme} , \operatorname{Scheme})$
AlgebraicGeometry.AffineScheme.mkdefMathlib.AlgebraicGeometry.AffineScheme, function or constant
Wording
no wording
English
Let $X$ be an affine scheme. Then $\operatorname{mk}$ applied to $X$ and $h$ is an affine scheme.
Formula
$X \in \operatorname{Scheme} , \operatorname{IsAffine} (X)\vdash \operatorname{mk} (X , h)\in \operatorname{AffineScheme}$
AlgebraicGeometry.AffineScheme.ofdefMathlib.AlgebraicGeometry.AffineScheme, function or constant
Wording
no wording
English
Let $X$ be a scheme. Then $\operatorname{of}$ applied to $X$ is an affine scheme defined as $\operatorname{mk}$ applied to $X$ and $h$.
Formula
$X \in \operatorname{Scheme} \vdash \operatorname{of} (X):= \operatorname{mk} (X , h)\in \operatorname{AffineScheme}$
AlgebraicGeometry.AffineScheme.ofHomdefMathlib.AlgebraicGeometry.AffineScheme, function or constant
Wording
the affine scheme of f (from the name)
English
Let $f$ be a homomorphism of $X$ and $Y$. Then the affine scheme of $f$ is a homomorphism of $\operatorname{of}$ applied to $X$ and $\operatorname{of}$ applied to $Y$ defined as the homomorphism of $f$.
Formula
$f \in \operatorname{Hom} (X , Y)\vdash \operatorname{ofHom} (f):= \operatorname{homMk} (f)\in \operatorname{Hom} (\operatorname{of} (X), \operatorname{of} (Y))$

AlgebraicGeometry.IsAffineclassMathlib.AlgebraicGeometry.AffineScheme, predicate
Wording

AlgebraicGeometry.IsAffineOpendefMathlib.AlgebraicGeometry.AffineScheme, predicate
Wording
U is affine (from the docstring)
English
Let $U$ be an open of $X$. Then $U$ is affine, if the scheme of $U$ is affine.
Formula
$U \in \operatorname{Opens} (X)\vdash \operatorname{IsAffineOpen} (U):\Leftrightarrow \operatorname{IsAffine} (\operatorname{toScheme} (U))$
